# web-platform-tests' tree construction tests

Generated by `scripts/wpt_tree_construction.py` from web-platform-tests'
html/syntax/parsing/resources/*.dat, commit 4aa56d3beafc, under the
3-Clause BSD License (see NOTICE at the top of the repo). Don't edit
by hand.

One test: its file, its input, its fragment context (empty for a
document), its scripting (`on`, `off`, or `both`), and the tree
expected, in the files' dump format without the `| ` prefixes.

    export class TreeTest(
      public file: String,
      public input: String,
      public fragment: String,
      public scripting: String,
      public tree: String,
    ) {}

In functions rather than a module-level list, and in chunks, so each
backend compiles them easily.

        new TreeTest("tricky01.dat", "<html>\u{A}<font color=red><i>Italic and Red<p>Italic and Red </font> Just italic.</p> Italic only.</i> Plain\u{A}<p>I should not be red. <font color=red>Red. <i>Italic and red.</p>\u{A}<p>Italic and red. </i> Red.</font> I should not be red.</p>\u{A}<b>Bold <i>Bold and italic</b> Only Italic </i> Plain", "", "both", "<html>\u{A}  <head>\u{A}  <body>\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      color=\u{22}red\u{22}\u{A}      <i>\u{A}        \u{22}Italic and Red\u{22}\u{A}    <i>\u{A}      <p>\u{A}        <font>\u{A}          color=\u{22}red\u{22}\u{A}          \u{22}Italic and Red \u{22}\u{A}        \u{22} Just italic.\u{22}\u{A}      \u{22} Italic only.\u{22}\u{A}    \u{22} Plain\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <p>\u{A}      \u{22}I should not be red. \u{22}\u{A}      <font>\u{A}        color=\u{22}red\u{22}\u{A}        \u{22}Red. \u{22}\u{A}        <i>\u{A}          \u{22}Italic and red.\u{22}\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      color=\u{22}red\u{22}\u{A}      <i>\u{A}        \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <p>\u{A}      <font>\u{A}        color=\u{22}red\u{22}\u{A}        <i>\u{A}          \u{22}Italic and red. \u{22}\u{A}        \u{22} Red.\u{22}\u{A}      \u{22} I should not be red.\u{22}\u{A}    \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <b>\u{A}      \u{22}Bold \u{22}\u{A}      <i>\u{A}        \u{22}Bold and italic\u{22}\u{A}    <i>\u{A}      \u{22} Only Italic \u{22}\u{A}    \u{22} Plain\u{22}"),
        new TreeTest("tricky01.dat", "<html><body>\u{A}<p><font size=\u{22}7\u{22}>First paragraph.</p>\u{A}<p>Second paragraph.</p></font>\u{A}<b><p><i>Bold and Italic</b> Italic</p>", "", "both", "<html>\u{A}  <head>\u{A}  <body>\u{A}    \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <p>\u{A}      <font>\u{A}        size=\u{22}7\u{22}\u{A}        \u{22}First paragraph.\u{22}\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      size=\u{22}7\u{22}\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}      <p>\u{A}        \u{22}Second paragraph.\u{22}\u{A}    \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <b>\u{A}    <p>\u{A}      <b>\u{A}        <i>\u{A}          \u{22}Bold and Italic\u{22}\u{A}      <i>\u{A}        \u{22} Italic\u{22}"),

        new TreeTest("tricky01.dat", "<table><center> <font>a</center> <img> <tr><td> </td> </tr> </table>", "", "both", "<html>\u{A}  <head>\u{A}  <body>\u{A}    <center>\u{A}      \u{22} \u{22}\u{A}      <font>\u{A}        \u{22}a\u{22}\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      <img>\u{A}      \u{22} \u{22}\u{A}    <table>\u{A}      \u{22} \u{22}\u{A}      <tbody>\u{A}        <tr>\u{A}          <td>\u{A}            \u{22} \u{22}\u{A}          \u{22} \u{22}\u{A}        \u{22} \u{22}"),

        new TreeTest("tricky01.dat", "<TABLE>\u{A}<TR>\u{A}<CENTER><CENTER><TD></TD></TR><TR>\u{A}<FONT>\u{A}<TABLE><tr></tr></TABLE>\u{A}</P>\u{A}<a></font><font></a>\u{A}This page contains an insanely badly-nested tag sequence.", "", "both", "<html>\u{A}  <head>\u{A}  <body>\u{A}    <center>\u{A}      <center>\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <table>\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}      <tbody>\u{A}        <tr>\u{A}          \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}          <td>\u{A}        <tr>\u{A}          \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}    <table>\u{A}      <tbody>\u{A}        <tr>\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}      <p>\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}\u{22}\u{A}      <a>\u{A}    <a>\u{A}      <font>\u{A}    <font>\u{A}      \u{22}\u{A}This page contains an insanely badly-nested tag sequence.\u{22}"),
